# Graph Report - himmel  (2026-11-28)

## Corpus Check
- 6001 files · ~11,793,876 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 1117 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .jsonl 265, .out 137, .patch 106)

## Summary
- 65898 nodes · 137969 edges · 3348 communities (2331 shown, 1017 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3800 edges (avg confidence: 0.79)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `297224e7`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
